## Citable passages

*The original script is given exactly as Pass 1 quoted it, with every flag. The English is a plain gloss. Page references are PDF pages.*

1. **The commission (the author's voice).**
   > "حالاتِ عمارات و مزارات و مقابر و مساجد نواحِ لاہور مفصل تحریر کروں"

   "…that I write in detail the account of the buildings, mazars, tombs and mosques of Lahore's environs." This is the order under which he says he wrote (p. 28).

2. **Madho Lal Husain at Basant** (`madho-lal-hussain`).
   > "الی الان[OCR?] بروز بسنت انکی مزار پر سرود و سماع و رقص و رنگ اندازی ہوتی ہی"

   "To this day on Basant there is singing, sama', dance and colour-throwing at his mazar." (p. 58)

3. **The khanqah's grants and the state** (`madho-lal-hussain`).
   > "اس خانقاہ کے تمام معاف و واگذار [illegible] سرکار کی طرف سے حسب فرامین شاہان[OCR?] جغتائی[OCR?]"

   "All this khanqah's revenue-free grants … from the government, according to the farmans of the Chaghtai kings." (p. 80)

4. **The English settlement** (`madho-lal-hussain`).
   > "بوقت بندوبست سرکار انگریزی باعث کسی سہو کے یہ [illegible] ضبط ہوگئی[OCR?]"

   "At the English sarkar's settlement, through some error, this [illegible] was confiscated." (p. 89)

5. **Data Ganj Bakhsh's mazar** (`data-darbar`).
   > "جو لوگ چالیس روز یا چالیس جمعرات آپکی مزار مبارک پر حاضر ہو سکے جو مراد چاہی خدا سی پاوی سکے"

   "Whoever attends his blessed mazar for forty days or forty Thursdays receives from God whatever he wishes." (p. 165)

6. **The Sakhi Sarwar mela in Anarkali (the author's voice).**
   > "جس روز یہ میلا ہوتا ہے اوس روز شیطان کا بازار بڑا گرم ہوتا ہے"

   "On the day this mela is held, Satan's market is very brisk." (p. 255)

7. **Ranjit Singh strips Mian Mir's tomb** (`shrine-of-mian-mir`).
   > "سب کے سب مہاراجہ رنجیت سنگ[OCR?] نے ... اترواکر ... باغ کی بارہ دری مین جو بمقام امرتسر اوسنی خود تیار کراتی تھی لگوایا"

   "Maharaja Ranjit Singh had them all removed and set in the baradari of the garden he himself had built at Amritsar." (p. 313)

8. **Dara Shikoh buys the land** (`shrine-of-mian-mir`).
   > "کل کو کوئی تم سی یہہ نہ کہی کہ دارا شکوہ نی زور و ملاوٹ[OCR?] زمین ہماری چہین لی"

   "So that tomorrow no one says to you that Dara Shikoh seized our land by force." (p. 316)

9. **Bibi Wadi and Mauj Darya Bukhari.**
   > "چنانچہ اوس روز سے فیمابین سادات گیلانیان و بخاریان درمیان[OCR?] رشتہ داری موقوف ہوگئی اور حتی الآن[OCR?] موقوف ہی ہے"

   "So from that day marriage ties between the Gilani and Bukhari sayyids stopped, and they remain stopped to this day." (p. 351)

10. **Bibi Pak Daman** (`bibi-pak-daman`).
    > "یا الٰہی! زمین کو حکم دے کہ ہمکو امان دیوے" … "تمام بی بیان و کنیزیں سما گئیں اور پوشیدہ ہو ئیں"

    "O God, command the earth to give us refuge." … "All the Bibis and the maidservants were absorbed and hidden." (p. 357)

11. **Ranjit Singh's samadh under British upkeep** (`samadhi-of-maharaja-ranjit-singh`).
    > "اب تک منجانب سرکار و التہدار[OCR?] انگریز ... بخوبی عنایت ہوتا ہے اور مرمت ہمیشہ سال بسال ہوتی رہتی ہے"

    "To this day [expense] is granted by the English sarkar, and repair is always done year by year." (p. 574)

12. **Shah Jamal's dhamal** (the book's "حضرت شاہ جمال").
    > "باقی رہ گیا اور بقیہ پانچ منزلیں زمین میں غرق[OCR?] ہو گئیں"

    "[One storey] remained, and the other five storeys sank into the ground." (p. 691)

13. **Khanqah land under Sikh rule** (Dori Shah's site, later given to the author).
    > "حسب رواج عملداری سکہان تمام زمین معمولہ خانقاہ جو قدیم سی واگذار تہی ... قبضہ مین کر لے"

    "As was the custom of Sikh rule, he seized all the khanqah land that had long been released to it." (p. 776)

14. **A government burial ban** (the graveyard of Shah Abul Muali's tomb).
    > "اب گورستان متعلقہ روضہ حضرت شاہ ابو المعالی واقع ٹیلہ شہید گنج مین تدفین جدید بحکم سرکار ممنوع اور مسدود ہوگئی ہی"

    "New burial in the graveyard of Shah Abul Muali's tomb at Tila Shahid Ganj is now banned and closed by government order." (p. 778)

15. **The afterword on the book's scope** (Fazl-ud-Din, the printer).
    > "جامع احوال بزرگان ہنود و اہل اسلام ہے" … "صلح کلی کی یہہ حالت ہی کہ ہر ایک مذہب والی کی قدر و منزلت کی رعایت ہی"

    "It gathers the lives of the holy men of the Hindus and the people of Islam … its universal peace is such that the standing of the followers of every religion is respected." (p. 872)

## Doubts and review points

*Everything below is what Pass 1 recorded under Doubts, merged across the 79 chunks. Chunks are named as "c. NNN"; pages are PDF pages. Mappings are frozen at Pass 1: the caveats and declines below are reported, not reversed. No date is converted or reconciled.*

### Mapping inconsistencies and held identities for Rauf

**Held identity questions (never resolved at Pass 1 or here)**

- **langer-makhdoom.** Mapped as named in c. 026 on "سید برہان الدین[OCR?]", a murid of Mian Mir in a khadimi shajra (p. 299), with no Chiniot, no langar, no "مخدوم" and no date (Pass 1 also noted, from the index and not the book, that the row's figure_died is 1245 and the index gives Mian Mir 1635); in c. 040 on "موضع لنگر مخدوم عبدالکریم صاحب" (p. 448), where the book's makhdoom is "عبدالکریم" while the index's principal figure is Makhdoom Burhan-ud-din of Chiniot, and the chunk names no district or city and the river's name is illegible; in c. 041 on the bare "حضرت مخدوم برہان الدین" in a shajra between "محمد و طیب[OCR?]" and "محمد و [illegible] الدین متقی[OCR?]" (p. 450). Not mapped: Chiniot[OCR?] as a distance reference for Bahlul's seat (c. 004, p. 53); the village "موضع لنگے[OCR?]" (c. 041, pp. 449, 454, 456), which the book explains by Muhammad Fazil's lameness; "ساکن چنیوٹ[OCR?]" (p. 631) and the jami mosque "بمقام چنیوٹ" (p. 633) (c. 056); "ساکن چہروٹ[OCR?]", Wazir Khan's home town (c. 067, p. 734), which would be a langer-makhdoom toponym only if a reviewer reads it as Chiniot.
- **shrine-of-baba-shah-chiragh — does "Shah Chiragh" conflate two men?** Mapped as the book names him in: c. 001 (the ToC line "سید علی ہجویری[OCR?] چراغ شاہ[OCR?]", p. 6, mapped to both **data-darbar** and this row — one entry on two men, an epithet, or a misreading); c. 008 ("شاہ چراغ [OCR?]", p. 104, in an unreadable passage); c. 009 ("عبدالرزاق المشہور شاہ چراغ", Gilani, with the internal discrepancies listed under Contradictions); c. 012 ("شاہ چراغ صاحب قادری سلطان پوری", p. 151, and a tomb of "عبد الرزاق" near "کوٹھی شاہ[OCR?] چراغ", p. 154 — the chunk does not say these are one man, and the link rests on the flagged "شاہ[OCR?]"); c. 013 ("سیّد عبد الرزاق", famous as "سیّد لگی[OCR?]", never called Shah Chiragh in the chunk; the mapping rests on the name and on the next section's "مقبرہ[OCR?] شاہ چراغ صاحب" as a neighbouring landmark, p. 158); c. 026 ("سید عبد الرزاق" only as "خلف الصدق حضرت غوث الاعظم" in a shajra, p. 299, no tomb, no date); c. 047 ("حضرت شاہ چراغ صاحب", a descendant of Abd al-Qadir Sani of the Uch line, mazar "نواح لاہور", p. 527). The row also sits only inside the place-level Lahore list in c. 033, 038, 039, 045, 049, 051, 052 and 059. Left unmapped by Pass 1: "تکیہ سید چراغ شاہ چشتی" (c. 001, p. 1); "سید عبدالرزاق مکی[OCR?]" (c. 001, p. 1; "مکی[OCR?]" also echoes **peer-makki**); "فقیر چراغ الدین" (p. 126) and "سید چراغ شاہ" (p. 132) (c. 010); "خانقہ سید چراغ شاہ[OCR?]" inside the Miani graveyard (c. 016, p. 192); the living contemporary "سید چراغ علو[OCR?] شاہ صاحب چشتی و سبزواری" at Miani (pp. 205–207) and the unattributed "مسجد چراغ شاہ" (p. 204) (c. 018); "سید چراغ شاہ", a sajjada nashin of Shaikh Tahir's khanqah dated "۱۲۶۱[OCR?]" (p. 215), and "قادریہ رزاق شاہی[OCR?]" (p. 221) (c. 019); "عبد رزاق[OCR?]" in Mahmud Shah's Qadiri shajra (c. 039, p. 436); "سید عبد الرزاق" in the p. 482 chain (c. 044); "چراغ شاہ" / "سید چراغ شاہ", descendants in genealogies (c. 058, pp. 649, 650); "سید عبد الرزاق بن غوث الاعظم" in Shah Badr's genealogy (c. 060, p. 671); "حاجی شاہ المعروف چراغ شاہ", living mujawir of Shah Dami Shah (c. 061, p. 675); Syed Ali Shah "جنگلی[OCR?] چراغ شاہ", Gilani, north of the fort in the parade ground (c. 064); "سید عبد الرزاق پاک", made a khadim of Ghaus Azam (c. 071, p. 775); "چراغ دہلی" in the Bahadur Shah chronogram (c. 078, p. 854); "سید چراغ شاہ سرداری", father of a chronogram writer (c. 079, p. 873).
- **The two Shah Inayats.** Held per the brief. What the Doubts record: **shrine-of-shah-inayat-qadiri** is in the place-level Mozang bullets (c. 009, c. 018); in c. 019 "عنایت اللہ قادری" is matched on name only, with a grave near Pir Hadi Rahnuma's tomb that cannot be reconciled with the row, and the following death date "[illegible] سو اکاسی[OCR?]" has an unclear subject; "شیخ عنایت اللہ" (c. 059, pp. 660–661), dated to Alamgir's and Shah Jahan's time, was not mapped; "حضرت عبد اللہ شتاکی[OCR?]" with a maqbara north of "موضع مزنگ[OCR?]" (c. 068, p. 747) was not mapped (different name); in c. 046 Mozang was deliberately not extended to this row.
- **Bulleh Shah's death year.** Held per the brief. No Doubts line in the extract bears on it; **mazar-of-bulleh-shah** is reached only by the place-level Kasur bullets (below).
- **Madho and Hussain as two men under one row.** **madho-lal-hussain**'s principal figure combines both; the book titles Madho "حضرت شیخ مادہو" (c. 004) and treats him as Hussain's successor and a separate person (c. 006). Madho's facts sit under the same row as shrine history; Pass 1 asks that the two men be kept apart.

**Chunk-to-chunk inconsistencies in how Pass 1 treated the same name or place (each chunk's treatment is reproduced as it stands; choosing one is Rauf's decision)**

- **Shahid Ganj.** Mapped to **gurdwara-shaheed-ganj-singh-singhnian** in c. 010 on the name "شہید گنج" only (the book does not say "Singh Singhnian", names no women or children, and uses "شہید گنج" for three things: the area with Mir Mannu's tomb, p. 121; the Sikh samadh near Sarai Mian Muhammad Sultan, pp. 122–123; a graveyard mound south of Abul Maali's tomb, p. 126). Left unmapped in: c. 011 ("گنج سکھاں[OCR?]" and "شہید سکھاں[OCR?]", p. 144); c. 019 ("مسجد شہید گنج والہ", a mosque, p. 217); c. 054 ("شہید گنج[OCR?]", Qutb Shah's residence, p. 606); c. 065 (pp. 718, 719, the place only); c. 070 ("شہید گنج" outside the Delhi Gate with "بہای تارا سنگہ", p. 766 — the book prints "تارا", not Taru; either **gurdwara-shaheed-ganj-singh-singhnian** or **gurdwara-shaheed-bhai-taru-singh**, or neither); c. 079 (p. 866, Bhai Bhag Singh came from there; either Lahore Shahid Ganj row or neither). In c. 071 the book places Abul Maali's graveyard at "ٹیلہ شہید گنج" (p. 778).
- **Ayaz.** Mapped to **darbar-malik-ahmad-ayaz** in c. 001 (ToC "خواجہ ایاز", p. 6 — a mosque, not a mazar; the title "خواجہ" differs from the index's "Malik Ahmad Ayaz / Malik Ayaz"), c. 073 (the bare "ایاز" advising Sultan Mahmud at Somnath, p. 805) and c. 079 (a simile, "اپنا ایاز"). Not mapped in c. 064 ("خواجہ ایاز[OCR?]", supervisor of the Eidgah bazaar under Jahangir, p. 709), c. 066 ("Khwaja Ayaz" / "محمد ایاز", Shah Jahan's reign at Shahjahanabad, pp. 727–728) and c. 075 ("خواجہ ایاز [OCR?]", Nur Jahan's father, a Tatar who served Akbar, pp. 826–827). Also not reached where Sultan Mahmud is named without Ayaz (c. 010, p. 127; c. 014, p. 170; c. 032; c. 074, pp. 806–807), by the Lahore toponym (c. 045), or by the Shah Alami gate (c. 050, p. 562). Note that c. 001 mapped a "خواجہ ایاز" while c. 064/066/075 declined three "خواجہ ایاز" forms.
- **"گجرات".** Mapped to **shrine-of-hazrat-shah-daula-daryai** as a bare toponym in c. 079 (an official's posting; "not said to be the Punjab town"). Not mapped in c. 020 ("ضلع گجرات", Mehr Shah's village, p. 227), c. 044 ("ضلع گجرات", pp. 484–485), c. 054 ("علاقہ گجرات", p. 606), c. 063 (pp. 698–699, tied to Wajih-ud-Din Gujarati), c. 073 (pp. 802, 803, early Arab campaigns — recorded by Pass 1 as a possible deviation from ruling (a)) and c. 076 (pp. 833, 837–838, mapped to nothing).
- **Bare "قصور".** Mapped place-level to **mazar-of-bulleh-shah** in c. 059 (flagged "قصور[OCR?]", p. 661), c. 065 (near-illegible passage; neither Bulleh Shah nor the shrine named) and c. 073 (founding form "کشور [OCR?]"). Not mapped in c. 024 ("لب سڑک قصور", a road name, p. 280), c. 044 (a residence, p. 482) and c. 070 ("نواح قصور", where Mir Mannu rode).
- **Miani graveyard** (**ghazi-ilm-din-shaheed**, **khawaja-feroz-ud-din-gharib-nawaz**, **tahir-bandagi-qadri**, **wasif-ali-wasif**). Mapped place-level in c. 001 (p. 1, on "میانے[OCR?]" / "خطہ میانی"), c. 016 (p. 187), c. 018, c. 019 and c. 067 (the Parsi graveyard). Not mapped in c. 005 ("گورستان سیانی[OCR?]", p. 64), c. 017 (the graveyard's land statement spelled "مہمانی[OCR?]", p. 193 — the four rows could take it if a human confirms the reading), c. 030 (pp. 339, 345, road and direction), c. 033 (p. 371), c. 041 ("لون[OCR?] میانی[OCR?]", p. 456), c. 047 (pp. 523, 525) and c. 054 (p. 603). The book never prints "میانی صاحب" except in c. 030.
- **Mozang** (**gurdwara-chhevin-patshahi-mozang**, **shrine-of-shah-inayat-qadiri**). Mapped place-level in c. 009 and c. 018 (the founder passage, p. 207 — Pass 1 says a consolidator may treat it as mohalla-as-location and drop it). Not mapped in c. 003, 019, 024, 027, 030, 033, 036, 042, 058, 060 and 068 (see Archive mappings and declines).
- **Ichhra** (**shah-jamal**, **shrine-of-baba-shah-kamal**). Mapped place-level in c. 025 (flagged "موضع اچہرہ[OCR?]", p. 287). Not mapped in c. 042, 044, 050, 062, 070, 072 and 073.
- **Baghbanpura and Shalimar → madho-lal-hussain.** Baghbanpura applied place-level in c. 001 (p. 6) and c. 066; Baghbanpura and Shalimar used as matching location in c. 003 and c. 008. Baghbanpura not applied in c. 056, 065, 071 and 072; Shalimar not applied in c. 001 (p. 7), 016, 031, 035, 039, 042, 071, 072, 076 and 078.
- **Lahore Fort** (**loh-temple-lava-temple**, **samadhi-of-maharaja-ranjit-singh**). Mapped place-level in c. 053 ("محاصرہ قلعہ لاہور", p. 589) and c. 072 (the book's building is inside the Fort; the index puts the samadhi "near" it). Declined in c. 008, 015, 025, 026, 040, 042, 045, 046, 052, 057, 058, 059 and 071.
- **Multan, Uch, Peshawar, Sialkot, Gujranwala, Sharaqpur, Sheikhupura.** Each was mapped place-level in some chunks and declined as a residence, origin, route or title in others (detail under Archive mappings and declines). The decline practice cites "the chunk 015 precedent" for Peshawar; c. 052 notes that its own Uch bullet departs from it. Sharaqpur: declined c. 070, mapped c. 079. Sheikhupura: mapped to **mausoleum-of-waris-shah** in c. 053, declined in c. 063, and listed as having no archive rows in c. 035.
- **Mian Mir as a place name.** Mapped (thinly) in c. 032 on a flagged road destination "میاں میر[OCR?]" (p. 360) and in c. 053 on "جو میاں میر کو حاصل ہے" (p. 593, saint or place not determinable); declined as road, locality or cantonment in c. 010 (p. 121), 019, 038 (p. 415), 042 (p. 462), 043 (p. 481), 044 (p. 492) and 079 (p. 869).
- **Unnamed "مہاراجہ".** Generally not mapped to **samadhi-of-maharaja-ranjit-singh** when unnamed (c. 002, 006, 015, 018, 022, 023, 024, 030, 031, 037, 042, 048, 054, 056, 068, 071), but c. 052 attaches the one-eyed "مہاراج صاحب" anecdote (p. 585) because p. 579 says Ranjit Singh lost an eye, and c. 070 and c. 079 identify "مہاراجہ کلان" with him through context.
- **Guru Nanak by the many-row rule.** Mapped on bare or garbled names in c. 045, 050, 058 and 060, but "بابا نانک" in a garbled place phrase (c. 026, p. 298) was not mapped — flagged by Pass 1 as a possible departure from the many-row person rule — nor "با و اناک ا[OCR?] ور" (c. 059, p. 658).
- **Shah Sulaiman.** "شاہ سلیمان نوری" mapped to **purana-bhalwal** in c. 042 (p. 467); "شاہ سلیمان" (c. 026, p. 299) and "حضرت شاہ سلیمان" / "حضرت سلیمان قادری[OCR?]" (c. 044) not mapped.
- **Taunsa.** **darbar-hazrat-khawaja-shah-muhammad-sulaiman-taunsvi-r-a** mapped in c. 069 on flagged "سکنہ" and "تونسہ" (p. 758); similar Khwaja Sulaiman mentions not mapped in c. 020 (p. 235) and c. 066 (p. 725).
- **Chah Miran.** "کھوئی[OCR?] میراں[OCR?]" (c. 001, p. 3) was judged "not the index's Chah Miran"; c. 036 leaves open whether "کھوئی میراں" (glossed in the book "چاہ خرد [OCR?]") is the row's Chah Miran; c. 064 does not identify "موضع میران دی کھوئی[OCR?]" with Chah Miran.
- **Other chunk-level deviations Pass 1 asked the coordinator to confirm:** "علاقہ بہاولپور[OCR?]" not mapped to the Bahawalpur rows (c. 011, p. 138); "جگن ناتہہ" not mapped to **jagannath-temple-sialkot** (c. 074, p. 809, possible deviation from the person rule); "شاہ پور" in the farman's seal block not mapped to **shah-yousuf** (c. 053, p. 600).

### Archive mappings and declines

*Every caveat Pass 1 attached to a mapping it made, and every mapping it declined, by id. Declines are not reversed here.*

**Place-level toponym bullets (ruling (a))**

- **Lahore (35 ids), place-level.** Attached in c. 002 (the survey's commission and scope), c. 003 (rests only on the section heading, p. 45), c. 007 (two Ahmad Shah Abdali passages, pp. 88, 91; the second a legend), c. 009 (the p. 111 Gujar Singh sentence is heavily damaged), c. 010 (only Mir Mannu's governorship and the order against Sikhs, p. 121), c. 011 (p. 142), c. 014 (nazim-of-Lahore material on Mir Momin Khan; the Mochi Gate / Kashmiri Bazaar clause is a location only), c. 017 (a city title "دارالسلطنتہ" in a heavily flagged line, and Shahjahan staying in Lahore), c. 019 (the Abdali massacre narrative in Shah Kaku's section), c. 022 ("شہر برباد ہوگیا", p. 251, read as Lahore from the following sentence), c. 024 (the city-wide ta'zia proclamation and the Shakir Shahi kunda), c. 026 (pp. 292, 296, 298), c. 029 (one damaged sentence, "پیشتر [OCR?] لاہور کے تین [OCR?] حاکم", p. 326), c. 033 (Sikh plunder after Abdali, "حاکم[OCR?] لاہور", "اندروں حصار بلدہ لاہور[OCR?]", p. 372), c. 038 ("ضلع لاہور", p. 414; "خندق لاہور", p. 423; garbled "حاکماں شہر لاہور", p. 420), c. 039 (the Khulasat al-Tawarikh account and Ranjit Singh's cantonments, pp. 430–431), c. 045 (the twelve-gates list and the subadari, pp. 497–498; the gates are not mapped separately to `bhati gate` or `shah alam market` rows), c. 047 (pp. 523, 525), c. 049 (p. 544, with "بڑہ[OCR?] کہتر[OCR?]" uncertain, and the garbled "لاہور وغیرہ" / "حضرات لاہور و امرتسر", p. 541; "لاہور سی", p. 540, not used), c. 051 (the garbled old-city-wall line, p. 572; "ضلع لاہور" and "شرقی لاہور", p. 575; the Wazir Khan baradari line carried in the same bullet only because it sits in the misl passage), c. 052 (Sikh-period political history), c. 053 ("سرکار لاہور" and "ریاست لاہور", p. 591, mean the Sikh state, carried as printed), c. 058 (garbled passages), c. 059 (one legend sentence with the flagged founder's name "لو[OCR?]", p. 661), c. 065 (Mughal and Sikh governance and Begumpura), c. 066, 067, 068, c. 069 (the visit to "تمام مزارات" and a poetic line), c. 072, c. 073 (pre-Islamic and Ghaznavid history), c. 074 ("یہہ ملک", p. 809, read as continuing p. 808 — a judgement), c. 075, 076, 077, 078 and 079. No Lahore-in-general bullet (Lahore only as setting, residence, title or distance) in c. 004, 005, 006, 008, 012, 013, 015, 016, 018, 020 ("جب شہر ویران ہو گیا", p. 227, about a mosque's decline), 021, 023, 025, 027 (borderline: "دروازہ شہر بہی کہلا رہتا ہی", p. 305, about the mela), 028, 030 (the Ravi's course, p. 342, about the garden's site), 031, 032 (the "قتل عام", p. 359, names no city), 034 ("لاہور میں … دفن", p. 376, illegible subject), 035, 036, 037, 040 (the Abdali plunder, p. 446, names no place legibly), 041, 042, 043, 044 (p. 492 is about the baradari), 046, 048, 050, 054 ("جب پشور[OCR?] [illegible] ساکنان [illegible] لاہور", p. 606, and "شہر بھر ولی لاہور", p. 605, too damaged), 055, 056 (p. 633 about one man's buildings; garbled "ریاست لاہور", p. 630), 057, 060, 061, 062, 063, 064, 070 and 071; in c. 051 the Than Bhairo house is not attached.
- **Lahore district rows: declined** for "ضلع لاہور" (c. 041, p. 456, a comparison), "تحصیل لاہور" (c. 053, p. 595, a residence) and "ضلع لاہور" (c. 054, p. 612, a village's location).
- **Lahore Fort, place-level:** **loh-temple-lava-temple**, **samadhi-of-maharaja-ranjit-singh** attached in c. 053 (p. 589) and c. 072. Declined: "قلعہ شاہی [OCR?]" (c. 008, p. 103); "قلعہ" by "دروازہ روشنائی" (c. 015, p. 179); "قلعہ لاہور" (c. 025, p. 291; c. 026, p. 292; c. 042, p. 462; c. 057, p. 638, "سیر[OCR?] قلعہ لاہور"; c. 058, p. 653; c. 071, p. 774); "قلعہ" with illegible qualifier (c. 040, p. 447); "قلعہ شاہ" (c. 045, p. 498); "قلعہ" (c. 046, p. 516); "سمن برج قلعہ لاہور" (c. 052, p. 582); the road "جو انارکلی سے قلعہ کو آئی ہے" (c. 059, p. 662).
- **Multan (8 ids), place-level:** attached in c. 002 (a conquest whose agent is illegible or unnamed — Pass 1 asks whether a conquest notice belongs to the Multan rows at all), c. 010 (the conquest and the fate of its Nawabs, recorded at their Lahore graves), c. 023 (all from inside the Sakhi Sarwar legend: ruler, fort, jail, gate), c. 052 (the "1818[OCR?]" conquest), c. 053 ("ناظم ملتان" and the killings of Agnew[OCR?] and Kahn[OCR?] Singh, p. 592; where they happened is not stated), c. 065, c. 073, c. 074 ("اکثر ملتان [OCR?]", p. 811), c. 075 (Multan's governor invited Babur, p. 817), c. 077 ("ملتان کا صوبہ دار", the Mughal suba) and c. 078 ("خطہ" flagged). Declined: c. 005 (p. 68, site-specific to the Zakariya khanqah); c. 007 ("شیخ ارزانی[OCR?]" with a mazar in "ملتان[OCR?]", p. 94); c. 014 ("نواب ملتان", p. 169); c. 018 ("موضع احمد پور علاقہ ملتان", "صوبہ ملتان", p. 205); c. 022 (distance and route, p. 256); c. 023 (the unnamed "ولی کامل" near the fort, p. 260, not mapped to any Multan row); c. 025 ("ملتان[OCR?]", p. 284, too garbled to say it is Multan); c. 026 ("سڑک آہنی ریل ملتان", p. 295); c. 027 ("سٹرک ریلوی ملتان [OCR?]", p. 305); c. 028 (p. 316); c. 029 ("ملتانی" weavers, pp. 329–330); c. 030 (p. 339); c. 033 ("تا ملتان", the Beas, p. 367); c. 036 ("زبان ملتانی", p. 396); c. 037 ("ملتانی" nisba, p. 404); c. 041 (the Muhammad Khalil mosque story, "زاہدان[OCR?] ملتان[OCR?]", p. 454); c. 045 ("سڑک آہنی ملتان", p. 497); c. 047 (p. 526); c. 048 ("مقام ملتان", p. 534); c. 052 ("ملتان کی طرف", p. 576, left under Other); c. 055 ("ملتان شام[OCR?]", a personal name, p. 616); c. 056 ("ساکن ملتان", "شہر وارد ملتان", p. 635); c. 060 ("ملتانی[OCR?]", p. 669).
- **Peshawar (10 ids), place-level:** attached in c. 014 (weak: a single clause naming the conquest as the occasion of a gift) and c. 073. - **Miani graveyard** **ghazi-ilm-din-shaheed**, **khawaja-feroz-ud-din-gharib-nawaz**, **tahir-bandagi-qadri**, **wasif-ali-wasif**: place-level in c. 001 (match by name only, flagged), c. 016 (book's "میانی" / "میانی پنج ڈھیر[OCR?]"; rests on the index's placement of **tahir-bandagi-qadri** and the book burying Shaikh Tahir "بمقام میانی", p. 187), c. 018 ("گورستان میانی" / "قبرستان میانی" / "خطہ میانی" / "سیانی[OCR?]"; the book does not distinguish this tract's boundaries from the later graveyard), c. 019 ("میانی[OCR?]", "صاحب" not printed; describes the graveyard in the book's own time) and c. 067 (the Parsi graveyard and the author's cross-reference). Declines as listed under the inconsistencies above.
- **Mozang** **gurdwara-chhevin-patshahi-mozang**, **shrine-of-shah-inayat-qadiri**: place-level in c. 009 (neither site named; first spelling "مرنگ [OCR?]", p. 110) and c. 018 (the garbled founder passage "پہ پر فرزیہ[OCR?] مزنگ کی ہو جہ بانی محلہ مزنگ کا نہا[OCR?] … مسمی فتح[OCR?] مزنگ", p. 207; subject Shah Mir or someone named Fateh[OCR?] Mozang). **Person-level bullets (many-row rule)**

- **Guru Nanak (18 rows), person-level:** c. 024; c. 045 (bare name in a garbled succession line, p. 498); c. 046 (mostly bare table data); c. 047 (pictures in the samadh niche, p. 520; flagged Gurmukhi words, p. 517); c. 050 ("گورو[OCR?] [illegible] نانک[OCR?] جی[OCR?]", p. 552; the speaker is illegible both times and the author himself doubts the exchange); c. 058 ("با نا نک", p. 654, garbled line); c. 060 ("بابا نانک" as an example of the Bedi caste name, p. 665); c. 061; c. 062 (birthplace "تلونڈی[OCR?] علاقہ لاہور" and death place "کیرت پور[OCR?] ... ڈیرہ نانک" not identified with any archive site); c. 072 (wall-painting list); c. 077 (a single sentence on his birth in Babur's time). **gurdwara-darbar-sahib-kartarpur** is not singled out (c. 024, c. 046). Declined: c. 026, c. 059 (above); "نانک [OCR?] فقیر" (c. 029, p. 327); "دہرم سنگھ[OCR?] نانک پنتھی[OCR?]" (c. 060, p. 669).

- **Guru Hargobind (6 rows), person-level:** c. 045, 046, 047, 061, 062. In c. 024 only **gurdwara-chhevin-patshahi-mozang** was mapped, because the chunk names that site in Mozang. **gurdwara-panjvi-chati-patshahi** appears under both the Hargobind and the Arjan bullets (c. 047).
- **gurdwara-guru-ram-das-ji, person-level:** c. 024 (bare name; Ram Das is placed at Amritsar); c. 045 (no Lahore site; "ساکن لاہور" attached to a garbled "ہر داس" line, p. 498); c. 046; c. 047; c. 061 ("خلف گورو امرداس[OCR?]", p. 680); c. 062 ("گورو [illegible] داس", then "گورو رام داس[OCR?]", p. 686).

- **Shiva (8 rows), person-level:** c. 029 ("لنگ [OCR?] شیوجی" in a non-archive shivala in a Lahore dharamsala, p. 326); c. 049 (Shiv ji in the jogis' origin legend and probably the p. 540 marble object; **chandragup-baba-chandragup**'s index figure is "Shiva (associated)"); c. 051 (jogi lore; "مہا دیو" only as the name of a burial thread, p. 568); c. 056 ("شیو جی کا لنگ[OCR?]", p. 635); c. 059 ("شیو جی کا لنگ" in a non-archive Lahore Shivala, p. 661); c. 074 (the Deccan, outside Pakistan). **Single-row mappings and declines**

- **bibi-pak-daman.** A bare landmark mention with the name flagged (c. 009, p. 107). "بی بی پاکداماں[OCR?]" (c. 011, p. 139); whether "خانقاہ حضرت بیوی[OCR?] صاحبان" (p. 137) is the same site is not stated. A seal only, "سجادہ نشین پاک دامنان [OCR?]" (c. 028, p. 321). In c. 031 the heading's "پاک دامناں[OCR?]" is flagged, and pp. 353–355 are read as still in the section only because no new heading appears. In c. 032 the chunk says "بی بی صاحبان" throughout; the only "پاکدامان" is flagged ("حضرات پاکدامان[OCR?]", p. 364); the mapping rests on the continuous narrative; no count of the Bibis is given. In c. 042 it rests on the graveyard named as a neighbouring location (p. 462) and the Alif Shah tradition, which the book reports as "عام مشہور" hearsay with its own caveat; Alif Shah is not the row's figure. Declined: "حضرت لی لی ما کلا مال[OCR?]" (c. 059, p. 660).
- **darbar-abul-muali-qadri.** Person rule on "خیر الدین[OCR?] شاہ ابو المعالی[OCR?]" with illegible context (c. 009, p. 116); a bare name used as a landmark, no location (c. 011, p. 134); a bare, flagged name in an illegible passage (c. 019); the name "شاہ ابوالمعالی صاحب" plus its position outside Mochi gate, no "Qadri", "ابوالمعالی[OCR?]" flagged (c. 031, pp. 346, 348, 352); "شاہ ابو المعالی", tomb "لاہور میں", author of "تحفہ القادریہ" (c. 033, p. 369) — the same section names a different-seeming Shah Abu al-Maali, son of Shah Nur[OCR?] and father of Shah Muqim (pp. 366, 369), and only the Lahore-tomb author is mapped; "شاہ ابوالمعالے[OCR?]", with the Sher Garh and Shaikh Daud[OCR?] link from the chunk itself (c. 043, p. 479); the printed "شاہ المعالی" in a badly damaged sentence (c. 058, p. 652); one flagged "ابو المعالی[OCR?]" in a near-illegible line, weak (c. 061, p. 676); "شاہ ابو المعالی" with the graveyard at "ٹیلہ شہید گنج" (c. 071, p. 778), while the index puts the site near Mochi Darwaza. In c. 010 the mela/death date conflict is under Contradictions.
- **darbar-hazrat-khawaja-shah-muhammad-sulaiman-taunsvi-r-a.** Mapped in c. 069 on flagged "سکنہ" and "تونسہ" in a garbled sentence (p. 758). Declined: "حضرت خواجہ سلیمان صاحب سنگر والی[OCR?]" (c. 020, p. 235, no Taunsa); "حضرت خواجہ سلیمان صاحب کر والا[OCR?]", khalifa of "حضرت نور محمد بہبل[OCR?]" (c. 066, p. 725); "حضرت شاہ سلیمان" / "حضرت سلیمان قادری[OCR?]" (c. 044, pp. 483, 486–488), which could equally be **purana-bhalwal**.
- **darbar-malik-ahmad-ayaz.** See the Ayaz inconsistency above.
- **data-darbar.** The joint ToC line with Chiragh Shah (c. 001, p. 6). Placement doubt in c. 006: the turban and cowrie passage (p. 78) names the urs of "علی گنج بخش ہجویری", but the "دستارِ بسنتی" and the Basant digression point to Hussain's shrine, and it is unclear whether "حسن[OCR?] علی شاہ[OCR?] سجادہ نشین" is the Data Darbar's or Hussain's — check the PDF before it goes to either entry. The opening dome and mosque (c. 014, p. 167) are placed here only because the same page names Hujwiri's mazar. The Shaikh Hamid / khanqah sentence is broken by "[illegible]" and "مقفل[OCR?]" (c. 015, p. 177). It rests on "ہجویری[OCR?]" and the garbled "حمیدیان[OCR?] … ہر یہ[OCR?]" (c. 018, p. 207). The bare title "حضرت داتا صاحب" in a damaged sentence (c. 020, p. 228). Locational and biographical only (c. 021; "سائیں جلی شاہ" is printed "حبلی[OCR?] شاہ" two lines later). Through the Pir Ghaib grave's location and custody (c. 025, p. 281). "دا[تا] [OCR?]" with a transcriber's restoration, with **peer-makki**, as a place where garden-wall traces are seen (c. 030, p. 342); "ہجویری [OCR?]" (p. 345) backed by "داتا گنج بخش" twice unflagged, and "حضرت کے عرس" (p. 345) read as Data's urs from the framing. Author-only, "ہجویری" flagged, the Kashf al-Mahjub quotation unreadable (c. 048, p. 537). Garbled forms "سر[OCR?] کنجش[OCR?] بجو بری[OCR?]", "وا نا گنج بخش[OCR?]" (c. 061). The book's name is "پیر علی گنج بخش", not "داتا" or "ہجویری" (c. 069). Declined: "کشف المحجوب" as a title in the source list, no author named (c. 002, p. 29); the inscription "اللہ محمد [OCR?] علی عثمان" on Hazrat Muhtaram's tomb (c. 038, p. 419).
- **loh-temple-lava-temple.** On the flagged "لو[OCR?]", read as Lava/Luv because the chunk makes him a son of Raja Ram Chand who built Lahore; no temple mentioned (c. 059, p. 661). On "لوہو", son of Ramchandar who founded Lahore; the chunk never says "Lava" (c. 073). Also in the Lahore and Lahore Fort bullets.
- **madho-lal-hussain.** c. 003: matched on "باغبان پورہ" and "باغ شالامار". c. 005: rests on "حضرت حسین" / "حضرت لال حسین" / "حضرت حسین لاہوری", the pairing with "مادہو/مادھو[OCR?]" and the Lahore setting; the chunk never writes "شاہ حسین"; a different "حسین" boy, a prisoner awaiting hanging (p. 64), is not the saint. c. 008: from "حضرت حسین", "حضرت مادہو", "باغباں پورہ [OCR?]" and "شالا [OCR?] باغ [OCR?]"; the full name is in c. 007. c. 028: a flagged "مادھو لال حسین [OCR?]" (p. 321) and the garbled "ما دہو لال[OCR?] حسن[OCR?]" (p. 318), a sajjada nashin's seal only. c. 029: a garbled clause (p. 333) — whose khadim Haji Jamil was ("دم[OCR?] رنگ[OCR?] بلاول[OCR?]") and whether "رنگ[OCR?] بلاول[OCR?]" is the sajjada nashin both depend on flagged words. c. 030: a bare name, "حضرت مادھو لعل دیوان" (p. 334), at the end of a flagged chain. c. 031: a flagged bare name used as a location (p. 348). c. 038: "مادھو لال حسین" (p. 417) and "مادھو لعل حسین [OCR?]" (p. 418), named as a khalifa of "حضرت بلاول دریائی [OCR?]" — whether this Bilawal Daryai is the Shah Bilawal of other sections is not stated. c. 040: the p. 440 phrase "مزار حضرت [illegible] و ہو [illegible] حسین[OCR?] صاحب" is damaged; the mapping rests on "حضرت مادھو لال حسین" (p. 442, twice) and "لال حسین" (p. 441); no Baghbanpura or Shalimar. c. 054: "حضرت مادہو لال حسین" (p. 610), "حضرت لال حسین" (p. 611), no site or date; p. 611 too damaged. c. 059: "مادہو[OCR?]" flagged on p. 662, clean on p. 663; the sajjada nashin's name illegible. c. 060: a bare name between illegible runs about a mohalla's name (p. 664). c. 066: "حضرت ماہ[OCR?] مولانا حسین مرحوم" read by Pass 1 as [مادھو لال حسین?] — a guess from damaged letters and the location north of Baghbanpura's Khwaja Ayaz mosque. c. 071: the deed's "شاہ علی حسین صاحب" (p. 781) may be a variant of "شاہ لعل حسین" (p. 780). Declined: "حضرت حسین" (c. 017; the book's own cross-reference "حضرت حسین کے خانقاہ کے حال", p. 195); Dori Shah's epithet "مادہو والی [OCR?]" (c. 071, p. 776); "لال حسینی" faqirs (c. 063, p. 700); "شاہ حسین" in "شاہ حسین زنجانی" is not this row (c. 001, c. 064).
- **peer-makki.** A candidate only in "سید عبدالرزاق مکی[OCR?]" (c. 001, p. 1, not mapped). Mapped by name in c. 061 ("حضرت پیر مکی[OCR?] صاحب"): the book places the grave in the south-east corner of the Company Bagh near the Taksali gate, while the index row has the Bhati Gate area (near Data Darbar) and principal figure Syed Azizuddin; the book gives no personal name, says his history is unknown, and offers only a speculative identification with Sa'd-ud-Din.
- **purana-bhalwal.** A bare name, "شاہ سلیمان نوری", in a shajra the author presents as Bhage Shah's (c. 042, p. 467). Declined: "شاہ سلیمان" (c. 026, p. 299); c. 044 (above).
- **ranmal-sharif.** The ToC "نوشاہ[OCR?] صاحب [illegible] گنج بخش[OCR?]" (c. 001, p. 4; both parts flagged, a word between illegible, "گنج بخش" alone is also Data's title). "روشن ضمیر حضرت نوشہ گنج بخش" in a chain, silsila "نوشاہی", no site (c. 030, p. 337). The bare "حضرت نوشہ صاحب" (c. 031, p. 349). "حضرت حاجی محمود شاہ نوشاہ گنج بخش" (c. 042, p. 467) — the given name "محمود" differs from the index's "Muhammad". "نوشہ/نوشاہ" alone in c. 044; the chunk never names Ranmal, and the burial statement (pp. 483–484) is broken across the page, so whose mazar is meant by "ضلع شاہ پور", "سہن پال[OCR?]", "رسول نگر[OCR?]" or "ضلع گجرات" is unclear; "حضرت حاجی محمد [illegible] بو شاہ گنج بخش صاحب" in Shah Farid's shajra (p. 483) is not used for mapping.
- **sakhi-sarwar.** Person rule on "سخی[OCR?] سرور سلطان" (c. 001, p. 2); the site named is a "چوکی" whose location is not stated. "لکہہ[OCR?] داتا" (c. 022); the Lahore chauki is not the row's site; the place of the mazar where lakhs gather is not named; "موضع شاہ[OCR?] سکوٹ[OCR?]" and "لگا[OCR?] ہا[OCR?]" not identified. "سید احمد سخی سرور", "سخی سرور صاحب", chronogram "سلطان عالم" (c. 023); Dera Ghazi Khan never named; martyrdom and grave at "مقام نگاہی[OCR?]", not tied to the row's site. A bare name, "سنگ" flagged (read by Pass 1 as the pilgrim band), "رہتی [OCR?]" flagged (c. 078).
- **samadhi-of-maharaja-ranjit-singh.** The ToC (c. 001, p. 4): only the first line names the samadh; the other four are person-level. Mapped by the person rule with the samadhi itself not mentioned in c. 007 (relations with the Madho Lal Husain shrine), 008 (stipends and darbar), 010 (every word of "مہا راجہ[OCR?] رنجیت[OCR?] سنگہ" flagged, p. 130), 013 (demolitions and building at other sites, pp. 159, 166; the p. 165 agent at Data Darbar printed "مہار[OCR?] ابہ[OCR?] زحمت سنگ" is not mapped or repaired), 014 (gifts and grants to the Hujwiri shrine), 018 (a dating point and patron), 021 (Sikh customs/revenue in a damaged sentence), 024 (pp. 274, 280; the city and the Imambara), 025 (named purely as a period, pp. 281, 282), 026 (Mian Mir's tomb and the stripping of stone), 028 (removal of marble; name flagged both times, "سنگ[OCR?]", "سنگہ[OCR?]"), 029 ("دوہار [OCR?] راجہ رنجیت سنگھ", p. 327; "راجہ رنجیت[OCR?] سنگ[OCR?]", p. 333; the fort wall "in his time" rests on "گرالی [OCR?]"), 037 ("بعہد رنجیت سنگھ", p. 413, clause broken), 038 (the Nura story, p. 423, "ایک [illegible] مورث مہاراجہ رنجیت سنگھ" — "مورث" may mean an ancestor; "اکال سہائی[OCR?]" flagged), 056 ("رنجیت سکہ[OCR?]", p. 630), 057 (p. 637; the "حضوری باغ" baradari named as the marble's destination), 058, 059 (the p. 659 comparison "دنسع[OCR?] یعنی سچر[OCR?] سمادہ مہاراجہ رنجیت سنگہ" uncertain; the p. 657 stipend passages damaged, "چہار احیہ[OCR?]" flagged), 060 (the samadhs of his wife or wives and of Kharak Singh's mother and wife, not his own; heading word "وجہ[OCR?]" read as wife; whether "نکائین[OCR?]" and "[illegible] کنور[OCR?] زوجہ رنجیت سنگھ" are one woman or two not clear, p. 665), 061, 062, 064 ("راجہ رنجیت سنگھ", "مہاراجہ صاحب"), 065 ("سرکار", p. 721, inferred to mean him), 068 (a single damaged sentence: praise despite "تعصب مذہبی"), 069 (a regnal date), 070 ("مہاراجہ کلان", pp. 768, 770, and bare "مہاراجہ", p. 768, identified through context), 071 (a reign), 078 (his actions at Jahangir's tomb and his vizier) and 079 ("مہاراجہ کلان", pp. 868, 870, not named as Ranjit Singh in the chunk; at p. 867 "رنجیت [OCR?] سنگہ [OCR?]" flagged with an illegible relation word). In c. 051 it is mapped on the section heading and margins, and Kharak Singh's and Nau Nihal Singh's samadhs, described in the same section as adjoining ("متصل"), are recorded under this row though they are not separate rows. Only in the Lahore place-level bullet in c. 022. - **shah-jamal.** The ToC "مقبرہ شاہ جمال[OCR?]" is the only Shah Jamal there (c. 001, p. 6). Place-level via Ichhra (c. 025). Mapped by name in c. 063 with the heading and location in c. 062 — confirm against c. 062. - **shrine-of-bahauddin-zakariya.** "بہاؤالدین[OCR?] ذکریا" (with ذ), about the khanqah's sajjada nashin of the day "شیخ کبیر[OCR?] بالا پیر[OCR?]"; the dream saint "حضرت جلدی[OCR?]" unplaced (c. 005). "بہار[OCR?] الحق زکریا[OCR?] ملتانی" in a chain (c. 037, p. 404). - **shrine-of-baba-shah-kamal.** Place-level via Ichhra (c. 025). A bare, flagged name "حضرت سید آنگ[OCR?] شاہ کمال" with a garbled subject (c. 033, p. 368). The word before "شاہ کمال" in the heading is illegible; identification by name and the place next to Shah Jamal's khanqah, while the chunk says "موضع راوان … لاہور سے دو کوس[OCR?]" and the index places the row near Ichhra (c. 059). A bare name, brother of Shah Jamal (c. 062). A bare name in a silsila (c. 071, p. 775). Declined: "سید کمال شاہ چشتی" outside the Kashmiri gate, d. "سمنت ۱۹۰۰[OCR?]" (c. 030, p. 339); Takia Kamil Shah, spelled "کامل" with a Madari chain, at "موضع بابو سا پور[OCR?]" / "بابو سابو" (c. 048).
- **shrine-of-fariduddin-ganjshakar.** A descent and adoption claim for the author's family, the adopted man "مرزا عنایت اللہ بیگ [OCR?]" (c. 002, p. 24). A bare name in a verse shajara and a chronogram invocation (c. 003). "حضرت بابا فرید الدین گنج شکر ... پاک پٹن[OCR?]", about the gaddi-nashins in a story set in Alamgir's time (c. 013, p. 161). A bare name at the end of a chain (c. 018). A bare name; relation to Shah Kaku illegible (c. 019). Only Farid's own deeds (c. 020); Farid Astana is a Lahore chilla site placed under Other — do not carry the Lahore mela into the Pakpattan entry. Silsila names (c. 031, p. 348; c. 039, "گنج شکر[OCR?]", p. 434). Pakpattan toponym (c. 069). - **shrine-of-mian-mir.** A contemporaneity statement (p. 125) and a cross-reference (p. 131) in c. 010. The book quoting Shah Muhammad Ghaus's own risala, a dream account (c. 015, p. 177). Principal-figure name only, from the Shah Bilawal anecdote (c. 016). A bare shajra head (c. 020, p. 227). "حضرت میاں میر" in a sentence broken by [illegible] (c. 021, p. 244); not mapped: "مزار حضرت میر صاحب" (p. 236) and "حضرت جناب جیا[OCR?] میر صاحب" (p. 237). A chilla in Sadr Bazar Anarkali that the book does not connect to the tomb; "وفات ... اسی حکمہ[OCR?] بر واقع ہوئی" and "خانہ[OCR?] ... اوٹھا[OCR?] کر لیکئی[OCR?]" flagged (c. 022). Pp. 310–312 attached only where Mian Mir or his sajjada nashins are named; the garden is Mulla Shah's site (c. 027); the burial "متصل عالم گنج" (p. 307) agrees with the row's "Alam Ganj (Dharampura)", and the book says Alam Ganj can no longer be traced. The grave register (c. 029) is mapped because its rows name his rauza, enclosure, murids, khadims and sister; whether every row is at his site is not stated. "میا میر[OCR?] صاحب" beside flagged "مرار[OCR?]" (c. 030, p. 336). "میاں میر[OCR?] لاہوری", a passing mention (c. 035, p. 389). "میا میر[OCR?]" (p. 442) and "میا میر" after an [illegible] (p. 444), locative or discipular only (c. 040). In c. 064 "میاں میر صاحب" is printed once (p. 702), then "حضرت شاہ میر" / "شاہ میر صاحب" / "حضرت میر صاحب" (pp. 702–703), bulleted under Mian Mir only provisionally. "میا نمبر[OCR?]" / "میا نمیہ[OCR?]" read as [میاں میر?] (c. 066). Declined: "میاں [illegible] میر تاجی[OCR?]" (c. 017, p. 200); the place uses listed under the inconsistencies.
- **shrine-of-miran-hussain-zanjani-zanjani-sahib.** "شاہ حسین زنجانی[OCR?]" on the name "Zanjani" (c. 001, p. 6); "سید یعقوب[OCR?] زنجانی[OCR?]" (p. 2) is a different person, not mapped. The bare "حسین زنجانی" (c. 013, p. 163). "سید حسین زنجانی" without "Miran", mazar location partly [illegible] ("نواح لاہور") (c. 024). "حضرت سید [OCR?] حسین زنجانی" (c. 036, p. 403); "میراں" appears only in the village name "کھوئی میراں". "شاہ حسین زنجانی" at "موضع میران دی کھوئی[OCR?]"; both year numerals illegible (c. 064). Declined: "میراں شاہ[OCR?]" (c. 016, p. 192); "میران بادشاہ[OCR?]" (p. 318) and "میران شاہ [OCR?]" (p. 321) (c. 028); c. 067 (above).
- **shrine-of-shah-rukn-e-alam.** A bare shajra name (c. 012, p. 154). The flagged "رکن عالم [OCR?]" inside "مسمی [OCR?] دریا [illegible] رکن عالم [OCR?]", a bare name in a chain (c. 036).
- **shrine-of-shah-shams-ud-din-sabzwari.** A judgement in c. 065 on "شاہ شمس تیرہ[OCR?] صاحب", set in Multan; the book does not name him Sabzwari. Declined: "شاہ شمس الدین" (c. 001, p. 3); the Shams-ud-Din Tabriz chronogram (c. 019, p. 221; why it is in the Pir Hadi Rahnuma section is unclear; a "شاہ شمس الدین[OCR?]" is named on p. 220); "شمس تبریز" (c. 024, p. 279); "حضرت شاہ شمس الدین[OCR?]" in a Lahore building context (c. 043, p. 481); "سیدان شمسی" / "سادات شمسی", once "تمسی[OCR?]" (c. 072, pp. 789, 791).
- **shrine-of-syed-musa-pak.** Declined: "شیخ موسی[OCR?]" (c. 001, p. 3); the Lahore smith-saint Shaikh Musa (c. 009).
- **shrine-of-hazrat-muhammad-ayub-shah-bukhari.** Declined: "ایوب شاہ بادشاہ[OCR?]" (c. 010, p. 125).
- **tahir-bandagi-qadri.** "Bandagi" spellings "مدگی[OCR?]" (p. 188) and "بندہ[OCR?]" (p. 191) read from context; the chunk makes him the Mujaddid's murid with the label "نقشبندی [OCR?]" next to an illegible name (p. 187) while the index says Qadri (c. 016). A name-match on "حضرت شیخ طاہر", never "Bandagi" or "Qadri" (c. 017); **shrine-at-odero-lal-udero-lal-teerath-asthan** also has "Sheikh Tahir" as a principal figure (Sindh) and was not mapped. "شیخ طاہر بندگی[OCR?]", "شیخ محمد طاہر صاحب", "شیخ طاہر صاحب"; no silsila, no date (c. 018). "شیخ طاہر صاحب [illegible]"; the "four" silsilas list only three names (c. 019). "طاہر بندگی[OCR?]" (p. 649) and "طا مر سدگی ہو[OCR?]" (p. 650), no location, date or "Qadri" (c. 058). Declined: "شیخ ظاہر[OCR?] محمدی" (c. 001, p. 1).
- **tomb-of-qutbuddin-aibak.** A bare dating point (c. 023). "قطب الدین عوری [OCR?]" / "قطب صاحب عوریہ" of the "سلطنت عوریہ", at Sadr Bazar Anarkali; the book never says "Aibak" (c. 024). The Delhi ruler "قطب الدین" who served Muhammad Ghori and had Shams-ud-Din as slave and son-in-law (c. 074, p. 809); two other Qutb-ud-Dins on p. 808 — "قطب الدین محمد غوری" and Saif-ud-Din's brother — must not be merged with him; no tomb mentioned. Declined: "قطب الدین غوری[OCR?]" (c. 001, p. 2); "سلطان ایبک[OCR?]" in the Chihil-tan list (c. 055, p. 618).
- **wasif-ali-wasif.** Declined: "مولوی نیاز احمد" (c. 069, p. 757); the row names a "Shah Niyaz Ahmad" only in its silsila column.

**Province-level bullets (ruling (c)) — every one "province-level, weigh accordingly"**

- **Punjab, written:** c. 005 (no boundaries; the first "پنجاب" flagged); c. 006 (the p. 84 instance concerns a boy in "ضلع امرتسر[OCR?]", outside Pakistan, recorded as wording only; the p. 80 instance badly damaged); c. 007 (weak: both statements, pp. 85, 95, incidental; "سرکار", p. 95, as printed); c. 011 ("ملک/ممالک پنجاب", no boundary); c. 012 (p. 145; the author's present, "دم[OCR?] تحریر", no year); c. 038 ("ہند و پنجاب", a Shah Jahan-period statement from "مرآۃ الہند", p. 415); c. 044 ("مسلمانان پنجاب" within a story, p. 484, no period); c. 052 (the Sikh "حکومت پنجاب"); c. 053 (the Sikh state under Dalip Singh, 1848–49 as stated, p. 592, flagged years; the Sutlej battles, Ferozepur, "دوآبہ[OCR?] جالندھر", Kashmir, "امرتسر" and "چنار گڑھ" not tagged Punjab; Saadullahpur and Chillianwala[OCR?] not located by the book; the farman's "صوبہ نجاب[OCR?] دار السلطنت لاہور", p. 600, a Mughal suba wording, kept out); c. 055 (a single dismissive phrase); c. 062 (period not stated); c. 066 (Shah Jahan period; the office title "نیر[OCR?] عمارت پنجاب" damaged); c. 071 ("پنجاب" under "عملداری سرکار ... انگریزی", p. 777); c. 072 (the famine, "ملک پنجاب" not bounded or dated beyond Mai Bhagi's childhood); c. 073 (Alexander-era "پنجاب", p. 799, not bounded; which of Porus or Abisares lived there is not said); c. 074 ("پنجاب [OCR?]", p. 814, following the book's regnal narrative); c. 077 (the 18th-century unit; the first statement also names Multan and Lahore).
- **Sindh, written:** c. 073 — "سندہ" means both the river ("دریائی سندہ", pp. 798–799) and the country ("ملک سندہ", p. 802); only clear country uses went in; "غرب رویہ سندہ" (p. 805) left out as ambiguous.
- **No province bullet, by Pass-1 decision:** c. 001 ("ملک", pp. 13, 15, names no province); "ملک ٹھٹھہ[OCR?]" (c. 005, p. 68); "پنجابی" as a language (c. 010, p. 121; c. 016, p. 191; c. 036, "زبان پنجابی", p. 402; c. 045; c. 046, p. 505); "تمام ملک ہندوستان و پنجاب" about Farid's devotees (c. 020, p. 234); "یہاں پنجاب میں ... دو سو آدمی", a follower count (c. 021, p. 237); "ملک پنجاب" in a register citation "یادگار[OCR?] چشتی[OCR?]" and "ملک ہندوستان" (c. 023, pp. 266, 267); c. 024; "Punjab" not named (c. 029, 051, 056, 065, 075, 076, 079); "ضلع امرتسر" (c. 030, p. 339); "فنانشل کمشنر بہادر ممالک پنجاب" (c. 031, p. 349); "میر بحر[OCR?] پنجاب" (c. 035, p. 392); "صوبہ لاہور", a Mughal unit (c. 039, p. 436); "ذکریا خان صوبہ پنجاب" in an inscription under "محمد شاہ" (c. 040, p. 441); "ملک پنجاب[OCR?]" and "فنانشل[OCR?] کمشنر پنجاب" (c. 047, p. 521); "سندھ[OCR?]" in Shah Madar's itinerary (c. 048, p. 537); "ملک" (p. 657) and "سرکار[OCR?]" (p. 662) (c. 059); "پنجاب[OCR?]" in a damaged phrase (c. 063, p. 700); "تمام ملک بجات[OCR?]" (c. 067, p. 738); "دریای سندہ" (p. 836) and "ٹہٹہ [OCR?]" (p. 838) (c. 076). Ranjit-era and Sikh-state wording such as "سرکار", "عہد[OCR?] سکھان[OCR?]", "بعملداری سکھاں", "سرکار خالصہ", "سلطنت دہلی", "سرکار ... انگریز" carried as printed (c. 029, 033, 036, 051, 054, 056). Places outside Pakistan take no province (Amritsar, Tarn Taran, Jalandhar, Ludhiana, Jagraon[OCR?], Kapurthala, Panj Graian near "پٹالہ[OCR?] ضلع گورداسپورہ", "ضلع گورداس پورہ", Batala, Ambala, Kangra, Sirhind, Malwa, Haridwar, Ajodhya, the Deccan).

### Contradictions

*Side by side, as printed. None is resolved.*

- **The author's pen name:** "المتخلص حسینی [OCR?]" (p. 27) against his poetic pen name "چشتی" (p. 24) (c. 002).
- **The author's father:** "والدم مولوی احمد حسن[OCR?]" (p. 21) against "مولوی احمد بخش" (pp. 22, 26) (c. 002).
- **Madho's conversion told twice:** "ظاہر بہی مسلمان ہو گیا" (p. 58) and "بصدق[OCR?] دل مسلمان ہو گیا" on the Ganga day (p. 59) — sequential or contradictory (c. 004).
- **Hussain's disciple categories and the second Diwan's tomb:** "با اسمِ عرس" and "شاہ عرس[OCR?]", then "شاہ عرب[OCR?]"; the third group "خانگی[OCR?]" (p. 82) against "خاکیوں" (p. 83); p. 82 puts the second Diwan's tomb inside the enclosure, p. 83 opens with "دیوان بخشو[OCR?] لعل[OCR?]" at Bijapur[OCR?] (c. 006).
- **Shah Chiragh / Abdul Razzaq (c. 009):** death margin "۱۰۶۰ [OCR?]" (p. 108) against "۱۰۹۲ [OCR?]" twice (p. 109); grandfather "عبدالقادر ثالث" (p. 108) against "عبدالقادر ثانی [OCR?]" (p. 109), with the father's father "محمد غوث بالا پیر [OCR?]" (p. 109); urs "ربیع الثانی [OCR?]" (p. 108) against death day "۱۷ [OCR?] ربیع الاول" (p. 109); "لنگر جاں [OCR?]" (p. 108) against "محلہ لنگر خاں" (p. 109). In c. 012 the order word is "سہروردی[OCR?]" (p. 154) against "قادری" (p. 151) and the index's Qadiri.
- **Abul Maali's death date:** the mela "۷[OCR?] ربیع الاول", called his death date (p. 126), against "سولویں ماہ ربیع الاول", the 16th (p. 130) (c. 010).
- **Abul Maali's father:** in "حضرت شاہ محمد رضا[OCR?] [illegible] کی جو خلف شاہ محمد فاضل[OCR?] صاحب والد حضرت شاہ ابو المعالی" (p. 124), Muhammad Raza[OCR?] or Muhammad Fazil[OCR?] (c. 010).
- **Shahid Ganj's Sikh-killer:** "میر[OCR?] مسعود[OCR?]" (p. 122) while the Shahid Ganj area is tied to "میر منو" (p. 121); the book does not equate them (c. 010).
- **Taj Shah's death:** "سنہ ۱۲۶۱[OCR?]" in figures against "بارہ سو سات[OCR?]" in words, alongside "سمت ۱۹۰۱[OCR?]"; age "ایک سو[OCR?] دو برس" (p. 135) (c. 011).
- **Rajab Ali Khan:** born "۱۸۷۲[OCR?]" (era not stated, p. 140), yet appointed "۱۸۳۴[OCR?] ء" and resigned "۱۸۵۳[OCR?] ء" (p. 141); also "سنہ ۱۲۳۳[OCR?] [margin] ۱۸[OCR?] ع" (p. 141) (c. 011).

### Names

- **c. 001:** the author's brother is "بہا[OCR?] الحق" and "بہار الحق[OCR?]" (p. 14), and "بہا[OCR?] الحق" in the p. 15 heading.
- **c. 002:** Ghulam Husain as "غلام حسنین [OCR?]" in one chronogram (p. 26) against "غلام حسین" elsewhere; the maulvi who died "۱۱۴۵[OCR?]" (p. 18) is not securely named (perhaps Maulvi Muhammad Ibrahim, p. 17); the relation between Ghulam Husain and the author's father is not clearly stated; "مسجد چینی والی [OCR?]" (p. 26) and "مسجد چینیان [OCR?] والی" (p. 32) may be the same mosque.
- **c. 003:** Miskin Shah goes to "مولوی باز احمد[OCR?]" (p. 35), but the death date (p. 36) is for "مولوی نیاز احمد" and the shajara has "نیاز احمد[OCR?]" (p. 37) — probably one man misread, not resolved; the chronograms are signed "سرور" and "غلام سرور[OCR?]" appears (pp. 37–38) — whether this is the author's full name is not stated; Madho Lal Hussain's caste "ڈھاڈا[OCR?]", descent name "کلشتر[OCR?]" and father "عثمان[OCR?]" all flagged.

- **c. 006:** the p. 84 boy is "میر احمد[OCR?]" and then "لور احمد[OCR?]"; who made the prophecy is illegible; the succession after Wilayat Shah[OCR?] partly illegible, with "خاند شاہ[OCR?]" / "عظمت شاہ[OCR?]" flagged.

- **c. 008:** the Begum's mosque inscription "بانی مسجد موران[OCR?] اور موران[OCR?] بیگم کی والدہ ہے" can mean the founder is Moran or the mother of Moran Begum (p. 97); "حضرت مادہو رہتی تھی[OCR?]" uses a feminine verb — do not build on it (p. 97); two sons "سید صفی الدین احمد [OCR?]" and "سید بہاؤالدین [OCR?]" (p. 103), "سید [illegible] الدین و سید بہاءالدین [OCR?] صاحبزادہ" (p. 104), a third son Shihab-ud-Din by the junior wife (p. 105), and "سید صفی الدین کلان" as Mauj Darya's father (p. 101) — check before building a genealogy.

### Structural and transcription artefacts

- **The table of contents (c. 001).** Every ToC number is printed text, not a PDF page; no ToC number may be used to locate a PDF page. The ToC begins at "۱۳۷", so anything printed before ۱۳۷ has no entry in the chunk.
